Q. What happens during the 'Fetch' stage of the CPU instruction cycle?
A) The next instruction is retrieved from memory location into the instruction register
B) The instruction is performed by the ALU
C) The output is written to the monitor
D) The power supply voltage is measured
Answer: A) The next instruction is retrieved from memory location into the instruction register

Q. What happens during the 'Decode' stage of the CPU instruction cycle?
A) The control unit interprets the opcode to determine required operations
B) The CPU shuts down
C) The hard disk is formatted
D) The printer is initialized
Answer: A) The control unit interprets the opcode to determine required operations

Q. What technique overlaps different stages of instruction execution to increase CPU throughput?
A) Instruction Pipelining
B) Clock Throttling
C) Thermal Throttling
D) Defragmentation
Answer: A) Instruction Pipelining

Q. What does EEPROM stand for in semiconductor storage?

C) External Energy Protected Memory
D) Embedded Efficient Processing Module
Answer: A) Electrically Erasable Programmable Read-Only Memory

Q. What is the name of the memory management technique where the OS uses hard drive space as an extension of RAM?
A) Virtual Memory (Swap space / Paging)
B) Cache thrashing
C) Overclocking

D) Run Application Module
Answer: B) Random Access Memory

A) Read Only Memory
B) Random Output Memory
C) Rapid Online Memory
D) Reset Operational Mode
Answer: A) Read Only Memory

Q. Which type of computer memory is volatile (loses its contents when power is turned off)?
A) Hard Disk
B) ROM
C) RAM
D) Flash Drive
Answer: C) RAM

A) Monitor
B) CPU
C) Hard Drive
D) Keyboard
Answer: B) CPU

Q. Which of the following is strictly an OUTPUT device?
A) Mouse
B) Scanner
C) Microphone
D) Computer Monitor
Answer: D) Computer Monitor

A) Plotter
B) Graphic Tablet / Stylus

D) Projector
Answer: B) Graphic Tablet / Stylus

A) Sound Card
B) Motherboard

D) Cooling Fan
Answer: B) Motherboard

Q. How many bits make up 1 Byte of data?
A) 4 bits
B) 8 bits
C) 16 bits
D) 32 bits
Answer: B) 8 bits

Q. How many Kilobytes (KB) are equal to 1 Megabyte (MB) in binary standard?
A) 100 KB
B) 512 KB
C) 1000 KB
D) 1024 KB
Answer: D) 1024 KB

Q. How many Gigabytes (GB) are in 1 Terabyte (TB)?
A) 100 GB
B) 500 GB
C) 1024 GB
D) 2048 GB
Answer: C) 1024 GB

A) 100
B) 101
C) 110
D) 111
Answer: B) 101

A) 01
B) 10
C) 11
D) 100
Answer: B) 10

Q. What is the decimal equivalent of the binary number '1000'?
A) 4
B) 6
C) 8
D) 16
Answer: C) 8

Q. Which logic gate gives an output of 1 (True) only when ALL of its inputs are 1?
A) OR gate
B) AND gate
C) NOT gate
D) XOR gate
Answer: B) AND gate

Q. Which logic gate inverts its input (outputs 0 when input is 1, and 1 when input is 0)?
A) AND gate
B) OR gate
C) NOT gate
D) NAND gate
Answer: C) NOT gate

Q. In Boolean algebra, what is the result of (1 AND 0)?
A) 0
B) 1
C) 2
D) Undefined
Answer: A) 0

Q. In Boolean algebra, what is the result of (0 OR 1)?
A) 0
B) 1
C) Undefined
D) -1
Answer: B) 1

Q. Which shortcut key is universally used to PASTE copied content?
A) Ctrl + P
B) Ctrl + V
C) Ctrl + X
D) Ctrl + B
Answer: B) Ctrl + V

Q. Which shortcut key is used to UNDO the last action?
A) Ctrl + U
B) Ctrl + Y
C) Ctrl + Z
D) Ctrl + R
Answer: C) Ctrl + Z

Q. What is the SI unit of electrical resistance?
A) Ohm
B) Volt
C) Ampere
D) Watt
Answer: A) Ohm

A) 11 ohm
B) 1 ohm
C) 0.5 ohm
D) 6 ohm
Answer: B) 1 ohm

Q. Which electrical device works based on the principle of Self-Induction?
A) Transformer
B) Inductor / Choke Coil
C) DC Generator
D) Electric Motor
Answer: B) Inductor / Choke Coil

Q. Fleming's Right-Hand Rule is used to determine the direction of:
A) Magnetic Force
B) Induced Current in a moving conductor
C) Electric Field
D) Magnetic Dipole Moment
Answer: B) Induced Current in a moving conductor

Q. Fleming's Left-Hand Rule is used to determine the direction of:
A) Induced current
B) Magnetic field around a wire
C) Force acting on a current-carrying conductor in a magnetic field
D) Electric field intensity
Answer: C) Force acting on a current-carrying conductor in a magnetic field

Q. In Fleming's Left-Hand Rule, which finger represents the direction of the Magnetic Field?
A) Thumb
B) Forefinger (Index Finger)
C) Middle Finger
D) Ring Finger
Answer: B) Forefinger (Index Finger)

Q. In Fleming's Left-Hand Rule, which finger represents the direction of the Electric Current?
A) Thumb
B) Forefinger
C) Middle Finger
D) Little Finger
Answer: C) Middle Finger

Q. In Fleming's Left-Hand Rule, the Thumb points in the direction of:
A) Motion / Force
B) Magnetic Field
C) Current
D) Voltage
Answer: A) Motion / Force

Q. In an AC Generator, which component maintains continuous sliding electrical contact with rotating coils?
A) Split-ring commutator
B) Slip rings and graphite brushes
C) Solenoid core
D) Permanent magnet poles
Answer: B) Slip rings and graphite brushes

Q. In a DC Generator, what component replaces slip rings to convert generated AC into DC output?
A) Split-ring commutator
B) Transformer core
C) Slip rings
D) Field magnet
Answer: A) Split-ring commutator

Q. A transformer has 200 primary turns and 1000 secondary turns. If the input voltage is 220V AC, what is the secondary output voltage?
A) 44 V
B) 1100 V
C) 2200 V
D) 110 V
Answer: B) 1100 V

Q. What safety wire in domestic household circuits carries leakage current safely into the ground?
A) Live Wire
B) Neutral Wire
C) Earth Wire
D) Phase Wire
Answer: C) Earth Wire

Q. What is the Commercial Unit of Electrical Energy consumed in homes?
A) Joule
B) Kilowatt-hour (kWh) or 'Unit'
C) Watt-second
D) Volt-Ampere
Answer: B) Kilowatt-hour (kWh) or 'Unit'

Q. What is the power rating formula for an electrical appliance operating at voltage V and carrying current I?
A) P = V * I
B) P = V / I
C) P = I^2 * V
D) P = I^2 / R
Answer: A) P = V * I

B) Von Neumann Architecture
C) Analog Architecture
D) Abacus Architecture
Answer: A) Harvard Architecture

Q. What is the fundamental execution cycle of any modern CPU?
A) Fetch, Decode, Execute, Writeback
B) Compile, Link, Run, Print
C) Install, Update, Boot, Shutdown
D) Format, Partition, Scan, Repair
Answer: A) Fetch, Decode, Execute, Writeback

Q. What is the term for running multiple processor cores on a single silicon chip to execute tasks simultaneously?

B) Virtual Memory Paging
C) Overclocking
D) Pipelining
Answer: A) Multi-core Processing (Dual-core, Quad-core, Octa-core)

B) Monitor and block unauthorized incoming/outgoing network traffic
C) Speed up internet download speeds
D) Clean physical dust from ports
Answer: B) Monitor and block unauthorized incoming/outgoing network traffic

Q. What does '2FA' stand for in account security?
A) Two-Factor Authentication
B) Two-File Authorization
C) Twice Fast Access
D) Total Format Algorithm
Answer: A) Two-Factor Authentication

Q. What is an algorithm in computer science?
A) A physical chip on the motherboard
B) A step-by-step procedure or set of rules to solve a problem
C) An optical mouse sensor
D) A computer monitor screen
Answer: B) A step-by-step procedure or set of rules to solve a problem

Q. A graphical representation of the step-by-step flow of an algorithm is known as a:
A) Pie chart
B) Flowchart
C) Bar graph
D) Scatter plot
Answer: B) Flowchart

Q. What does 'CPU' stand for in computer systems?
A) Central Processing Unit
B) Central Power Unit
C) Computer Program Unit
D) Core Personal Unit
Answer: A) Central Processing Unit

Answer: B) Random Access Memory

A) AND Gate
B) OR Gate
C) NOT Gate
D) NAND Gate
Answer: B) OR Gate

A) AND Gate
B) OR Gate
C) NOT Gate
D) NAND Gate
Answer: C) NOT Gate

A) AND Gate
B) NAND Gate
C) OR Gate
D) NOR Gate
Answer: B) NAND Gate

A) XOR (Exclusive OR) Gate
B) AND Gate
C) OR Gate
D) XNOR Gate
Answer: A) XOR (Exclusive OR) Gate

Q. What binary logic gate can be built by connecting an AND gate to a NOT gate in series?
A) NAND Gate
B) NOR Gate
C) XOR Gate
D) Buffer Gate
Answer: A) NAND Gate

A) NOR Gate
B) NAND Gate
C) XNOR Gate
D) AND Gate
Answer: A) NOR Gate

Q. Which built-in function in LibreOffice Calc calculates the arithmetic total of numbers in a range of cells?
A) SUM()
B) TOTAL()
C) ADD()
D) COUNT()
Answer: A) SUM()

Q. Which function in LibreOffice Calc calculates the arithmetic mean of a range of numerical values?
A) AVERAGE()
B) MEAN()
C) MEDIAN()
D) SUM()
Answer: A) AVERAGE()

Q. What formula correctly calculates the sum of all cell values from A1 through A10 in Calc?
A) =SUM(A1:A10)
B) =ADD(A1..A10)
C) =TOTAL(A1:A10)
D) SUM A1 to A10
Answer: A) =SUM(A1:A10)

Q. What process in computer animation automatically calculates intermediate frames between two keyframes?
A) Tweening (Inbetweening)
B) Rendering
C) Rasterization
D) Morphing
Answer: A) Tweening (Inbetweening)

Q. What physics formula is used to calculate obstacle distance using ultrasonic echo travel time (t)?
A) Distance = (Speed of Sound * Time) / 2
B) Distance = Speed * Time
C) Distance = Time / Speed
D) Distance = Speed^2 * Time
Answer: A) Distance = (Speed of Sound * Time) / 2

Q. What device measures atmospheric barometric pressure to calculate altitude above sea level in weather stations?
A) BMP180 / BMP280 Barometric Pressure Sensor
B) DHT11
C) LDR
D) MQ-2
Answer: A) BMP180 / BMP280 Barometric Pressure Sensor

Q. In Scratch, which block calculates the remainder after dividing one number by another?
A) () mod ()
B) () / ()
C) () * ()
D) round ()
Answer: A) () mod ()

Q. In Python, which arithmetic operator calculates the remainder of a division (Modulus)?
A) % (Percent)
B) /
C) //
D) **
Answer: A) % (Percent)

Q. Which keyword in Python is used inside a function to send a calculated result back to the caller?
A) return
B) send
C) give
D) output
Answer: A) return

Q. What built-in function calculates the sum of all numerical items in an iterable in Python?
A) sum()
B) total()
C) add()
D) accumulate()
Answer: A) sum()

Q. Two resistors of 6 ohms and 3 ohms are connected in parallel. What is their effective equivalent resistance?
A) 2 ohms
B) 9 ohms
C) 18 ohms
D) 4.5 ohms
Answer: A) 2 ohms

Q. Three resistors of 5 ohms, 10 ohms, and 15 ohms are connected in series. What is the total equivalent resistance of the circuit?
A) 30 ohms
B) 15 ohms
C) 2.7 ohms
D) 50 ohms
Answer: A) 30 ohms

Q. If three identical resistors of 30 ohms each are connected in parallel, what is the equivalent resistance of the combination?
A) 10 ohms
B) 90 ohms
C) 15 ohms
D) 5 ohms
Answer: A) 10 ohms

Q. A potential difference of 24 V is applied across an 8-ohm resistor. What is the electric current flowing through it?
A) 3 A
B) 192 A
C) 0.33 A
D) 16 A
Answer: A) 3 A

Q. An electric heater draws a current of 5 A from a 230 V power supply. What is the electrical resistance of the heating element?
A) 46 ohms
B) 1150 ohms
C) 225 ohms
D) 50 ohms
Answer: A) 46 ohms

Q. What voltage is required to produce a current of 0.5 A through a lamp of resistance 200 ohms?
A) 100 V
B) 400 V
C) 50 V
D) 200.5 V
Answer: A) 100 V

Q. Two identical 10-ohm resistors are first connected in series and then in parallel. What is the ratio of their series resistance to parallel resistance?
A) 4 : 1
B) 2 : 1
C) 1 : 4
D) 1 : 1
Answer: A) 4 : 1

Q. A 12 V battery is connected across two resistors of 4 ohms and 2 ohms connected in series. What is the potential difference across the 4-ohm resistor?
A) 8 V
B) 4 V
C) 6 V
D) 12 V
Answer: A) 8 V

Q. In a parallel circuit with two branches of 12 ohms each connected to a 12 V supply, what is the current flowing through each individual branch?
A) 1 A
B) 2 A
C) 0.5 A
D) 12 A
Answer: A) 1 A

Q. Four 100-ohm resistors are connected in parallel across a 50 V source. What is the total current drawn from the source?
A) 2 A
B) 0.5 A
C) 4 A
D) 0.25 A
Answer: A) 2 A

Q. An electric bulb is rated 100 W, 220 V. What is the electrical current drawn by the bulb when operated at 220 V?
A) 0.45 A
B) 2.2 A
C) 22 A
D) 1.5 A
Answer: A) 0.45 A

Q. According to Joule's Law of Heating (H = I^2 * R * t), if the current passing through a fixed resistor is doubled, how many times will the heat generated increase in the same time?
A) 4 times
B) 2 times
C) 8 times
D) Half
Answer: A) 4 times

Q. An electric iron rated 750 W is used for 2 hours daily. What is the electrical energy consumed in kilowatt-hours (kWh) per day?
A) 1.5 kWh
B) 1500 kWh
C) 0.375 kWh
D) 3 kWh
Answer: A) 1.5 kWh

Q. A 1000 W electric geyser runs for 3 hours. How many units (kWh) of commercial electricity are consumed?
A) 3 units
B) 300 units
C) 3000 units
D) 0.3 units
Answer: A) 3 units

Q. A current of 2 A flows through a resistor of 10 ohms for 5 seconds. What is the total heat energy generated in Joules (H = I^2 * R * t)?
A) 200 J
B) 100 J
C) 400 J
D) 50 J
Answer: A) 200 J

Q. An electric appliance rated 2 kW operates on a 200 V circuit. What is the current drawn by this appliance?
A) 10 A
B) 1 A
C) 100 A
D) 4 A
Answer: A) 10 A

Q. Which of the following consumes more electrical energy: a 250 W TV set operated for 1 hour or a 1200 W toaster operated for 10 minutes (1/6 hour)?
A) The 250 W TV set consumes more energy
B) The 1200 W toaster consumes more energy
C) Both consume exactly the same energy
D) Cannot be determined without voltage
Answer: A) The 250 W TV set consumes more energy

Q. A fuse wire rated 5 A is connected in a domestic 230 V line. What is the maximum power of an appliance that can safely run without blowing the fuse?
A) 1150 W
B) 2300 W
C) 500 W
D) 46 W
Answer: A) 1150 W

Q. A step-down transformer has 1000 turns in the primary coil and 100 turns in the secondary coil. If the primary voltage is 220 V, what is the secondary output voltage?
A) 22 V
B) 2200 V
C) 110 V
D) 44 V
Answer: A) 22 V

Q. In a transformer, the primary coil has 200 turns and the secondary coil has 800 turns. If the input AC voltage is 12 V, what is the output voltage (Vs = Vp * Ns / Np)?
A) 48 V
B) 3 V
C) 24 V
D) 96 V
Answer: A) 48 V

Q. In an ideal step-up transformer, the secondary voltage is 5 times the primary voltage. What will be the secondary current compared to the primary current (assuming 100% efficiency)?
A) 1/5 of the primary current
B) 5 times the primary current
C) Equal to primary current
D) 25 times the primary current
Answer: A) 1/5 of the primary current

Q. A step-up transformer converts 10 V to 200 V. What is the turns ratio (Ns / Np) of this transformer?
A) 20 : 1
B) 1 : 20
C) 10 : 1
D) 2 : 1
Answer: A) 20 : 1

Q. A convex lens has a focal length of +0.5 meters. What is the optical power of this lens in Dioptres (P = 1 / f)?
A) +2 D
B) +0.5 D
C) -2 D
D) +5 D
Answer: A) +2 D

Q. A doctor prescribes a corrective lens of power -2.5 D for a student. What is the focal length and type of this lens?
A) -0.4 m, Concave lens
B) +0.4 m, Convex lens
C) -2.5 m, Concave lens
D) +2.5 m, Convex lens
Answer: A) -0.4 m, Concave lens

Q. A convex lens has a focal length of +25 cm. What is its power in Dioptres (P = 100 / f_in_cm)?
A) +4 D
B) +0.25 D
C) +2.5 D
D) +25 D
Answer: A) +4 D

Q. An object is placed at 2F (twice the focal length) in front of a convex lens. What is the nature and size of the image formed?
A) Real, inverted, and same size as object
B) Virtual, erect, and magnified
C) Real, inverted, and diminished
D) Real, inverted, and highly enlarged
Answer: A) Real, inverted, and same size as object

Q. If a ray of light traveling from air enters water at an angle, which of the following is true about its speed and direction?
A) Speed decreases and ray bends towards the normal
B) Speed increases and ray bends away from normal
C) Speed decreases and ray bends away from normal
D) Speed and direction remain unchanged
Answer: A) Speed decreases and ray bends towards the normal

Q. A car accelerates uniformly from rest to a speed of 20 m/s in 5 seconds. What is the acceleration of the car (a = (v - u) / t)?
A) 4 m/s^2
B) 100 m/s^2
C) 0.25 m/s^2
D) 5 m/s^2
Answer: A) 4 m/s^2

Q. A stone dropped freely from the top of a building reaches the ground in 3 seconds. Taking g = 9.8 m/s^2, what is the final velocity of the stone on reaching the ground (v = u + gt)?
A) 29.4 m/s
B) 9.8 m/s
C) 14.7 m/s
D) 44.1 m/s
Answer: A) 29.4 m/s

Q. What is the kinetic energy of a body of mass 2 kg moving with a uniform velocity of 4 m/s (KE = 0.5 * m * v^2)?
A) 16 J
B) 8 J
C) 32 J
D) 4 J
Answer: A) 16 J

Q. If the speed of a moving car is tripled (3 times), how many times will its kinetic energy increase?
A) 9 times
B) 3 times
C) 6 times
D) 27 times
Answer: A) 9 times

Q. A constant force of 20 N moves a box across a smooth floor through a distance of 5 meters in the direction of the force. What is the work done (W = F * s)?
A) 100 J
B) 4 J
C) 25 J
D) 500 J
Answer: A) 100 J

Q. A sound wave has a frequency of 500 Hz and travels at a speed of 340 m/s in air. What is the wavelength of the sound wave (lambda = v / f)?
A) 0.68 m
B) 1.47 m
C) 170 m
D) 0.34 m
Answer: A) 0.68 m

Q. A student claps near a cliff and hears the echo after 2 seconds. If the speed of sound in air is 340 m/s, what is the distance to the cliff (d = (v * t) / 2)?
A) 340 m
B) 680 m
C) 170 m
D) 1360 m
Answer: A) 340 m

B) 20 J
C) 50 J
D) 100 J

Q. In digital electronics, what is the output of an AND gate when inputs are A = 1 and B = 0?
A) 0
B) 1
C) Floating
D) High impedance
Answer: A) 0

Q. What is the output of an OR gate when the inputs are A = 0 and B = 1?
A) 1
B) 0
C) Undefined
D) Null
Answer: A) 1

Q. What logic gate gives an output of 1 ONLY when both of its inputs are 1?
A) AND gate
B) OR gate
C) XOR gate
D) NOR gate
Answer: A) AND gate

Q. What is the output of a NAND gate when both inputs are 1 (A = 1, B = 1)?
A) 0
B) 1
C) High
D) Oscillating
Answer: A) 0

Q. What is the output of a NOR gate when both inputs are 0 (A = 0, B = 0)?
A) 1
B) 0
C) Low
D) Infinity
Answer: A) 1

Q. If a NOT gate has an input of binary 0, what is its output?
A) 1
B) 0
C) -1
D) Indeterminate
Answer: A) 1

Q. Which logic gate produces an output of 1 when its two inputs are different (e.g. 0 and 1, or 1 and 0), and 0 when they are identical?
A) XOR gate
B) XNOR gate
C) AND gate
D) OR gate
Answer: A) XOR gate

Q. What logic gate is formed by connecting an inverter (NOT gate) directly to the output of an AND gate?
A) NAND gate
B) NOR gate
C) XOR gate
D) Buffer
Answer: A) NAND gate
